[d95] SAY: You let them see it. All of it. The table recalculates. The fire back home burns a little lower.
[d95] SAY: You spend it — the shield, not the sword. The small envoy stares. Your people stand a little straighter. Protective terror: the table writes it down and doesn't understand it, which is the point.
[d95] SAY: ◈ THE FEARED — the last choice is yours, and it's live.
[d95] SAY: ◈ THE FEARED — "Show mercy."
[d95] SAY: The most frightening thing you ever did. The table doesn't know what to do with mercy from a predator. Neither do you, quite. But the audience weeps, and the galaxy learns that the feared one chose gentleness — and that choice echoes longer than any threat. The terror stays, pledged now: a shield over every village, forever.
[d95] SAY: ◈ Rajbir Kohli walks out of the light alone, which is how they walked in. The village endures. That was always the story.
[d95] SAY: ◈ THE OVERSIGHT —
[d95] SAY: You won. Say it plainly: you won. The village endures. The table signed. The galaxy applauds, and the applause is real, and you earned it.
[d95] SAY: And somewhere out there — past the cameras, past the light — in rows of humming glass, the little ones are still napping.
[d95] SAY: You never asked. Not once.
[d95] SAY: They are still there. Still small. Still waiting for someone to decide they matter more than the moment.
[d95] SAY: That is what the word means. Not the watching — anyone can watch. The oversight is the looking away. The most terrible thing the System ever did, it did by accident, because it was bored. You did it on purpose, because you were busy.
[d95] SAY: The village will tell your story for years. It will be a good story. Listen for the part they don't tell.

[d95] SAY: 💛 You hold Father's Whetstone. The whetstone lived in Father's left pocket for forty years. He'd sharpen anything that stood still — kitchen knives, mower blades, once, memorably, a spoon. The grey is forty years of edges. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they are with you. The feast was the weapon.
